/**
 * Les critères de la recherche avancée, dans l'ordre d'affichage.
 */
function bqb_search_fields() {
	return array(
		'collection'   => 'Collection / origine',
		'nature'       => 'Nature du document',
		'theme'        => 'Thème',
		'periode'      => 'Période',
		'secteur'      => 'Secteur',
		'pays'         => 'Pays / territoire',
		'personne'     => 'Auteur / personne',
		'organisation' => 'Organisation / source',
	);
}

/**
 * La barre de recherche de la page Bibliothèque. Une seule barre : en mode
 * simple, un champ libre ; en mode avancé, le même champ et tous les
 * critères. Un sélecteur « Simple / Avancée » passe de l'un à l'autre.
 * Le mode avancé s'ouvre d'office quand un critère est déjà actif.
 */
function bqb_render_search( $advanced = false ) {
	if ( ! bqb_library_url() ) {
		return '';
	}

	$url_f  = bqb_url_filters();
	$action = bqb_library_link();
	$val    = function ( $key ) use ( $url_f ) {
		return isset( $url_f[ $key ] ) ? $url_f[ $key ] : '';
	};

	// La période arrive sous la forme « 1990-1999 » ou « 2004 ».
	$range = array_map( 'intval', explode( '-', $val( 'annee' ) ) );
	$from  = ( $range[0] > 1000 ) ? $range[0] : 0;
	$to    = isset( $range[1] ) ? ( $range[1] < 2100 ? $range[1] : 0 ) : $from;

	$active = 0;
	foreach ( array_keys( bqb_search_fields() ) as $key ) {
		if ( '' !== ( 'periode' === $key ? $val( 'annee' ) : $val( $key ) ) ) {
			$active++;
		}
	}
	$advanced = $advanced || $active > 0;

	$field = function ( $key, $label, $control, $set ) {
		return '<div class="bqb-field' . ( $set ? ' is-set' : '' ) . ( 'periode' === $key ? ' bqb-field--period' : '' ) . '"><label class="bqb-field__label" for="bqb-s-' . esc_attr( $key ) . '">' . esc_html( $label ) . '</label>' . $control . '</div>';
	};

	$map      = bqb_short_map();
	$controls = '';

	foreach ( bqb_search_fields() as $key => $label ) {
		if ( 'periode' === $key ) {
			$years = bqb_years();
			$from_opts = '<option value="">Toutes</option>';
			foreach ( array_reverse( $years ) as $year ) {
				$from_opts .= '<option value="' . (int) $year . '"' . selected( $from, $year, false ) . '>' . (int) $year . '</option>';
			}
			$to_opts = '<option value="">Toutes</option>';
			foreach ( $years as $year ) {
				$to_opts .= '<option value="' . (int) $year . '"' . selected( $to, $year, false ) . '>' . (int) $year . '</option>';
			}
			$controls .= $field(
				$key,
				$label,
				'<div class="bqb-field__range"><select class="bqb-input" name="f_de" id="bqb-s-periode" aria-label="Période : à partir de">' . $from_opts . '</select>'
					. '<span aria-hidden="true">à</span><select class="bqb-input" name="f_jusqua" aria-label="Période : jusqu\'à">' . $to_opts . '</select></div>',
				'' !== $val( 'annee' )
			);
			continue;
		}

		if ( in_array( $key, array( 'secteur', 'pays' ), true ) ) {
			$select = '<select class="bqb-input" name="f_' . esc_attr( $key ) . '" id="bqb-s-' . esc_attr( $key ) . '"><option value="">Tous</option>';
			foreach ( bqb_filter_terms( $key ) as $term ) {
				$select .= '<option value="' . esc_attr( $term->slug ) . '"' . selected( $val( $key ), $term->slug, false ) . '>' . esc_html( $term->name ) . '</option>';
			}
			$controls .= $field( $key, $label, $select . '</select>', '' !== $val( $key ) );
			continue;
		}

		$all    = in_array( $key, array( 'collection', 'nature' ), true ) ? 'Toutes' : 'Tous';
		$select = str_replace(
			array( 'bqb-admin__input', '<select ' ),
			array( 'bqb-input', '<select id="bqb-s-' . esc_attr( $key ) . '" ' ),
			bqb_term_select( $map[ $key ], 'f_' . $key, $val( $key ), $all )
		);
		$controls .= $field( $key, $label, $select, '' !== $val( $key ) );
	}

	$docs    = bqb_count( array() );
	$persons = wp_count_terms( array( 'taxonomy' => 'bqb_personne', 'hide_empty' => true ) );
	$links   = array(
		array( bqb_library_link( array( 'f_groupe' => 'annee' ) ), 'Chronologie', 'calendar' ),
		array( bqb_library_link( array( 'f_annuaire' => 'personnes' ) ), 'Auteurs / Personnes', 'person' ),
		array( bqb_library_link( array( 'f_annuaire' => 'personnes', 'f_role' => 'laureat' ) ), 'Lauréats', 'quote' ),
		array( bqb_library_link( array( 'f_annuaire' => 'organisations' ) ), 'Organisations / Sources', 'building' ),
	);

	$html  = '<section class="bqb-search' . ( $advanced ? ' is-advanced' : '' ) . '" id="recherche-avancee" data-bqb-search aria-label="Rechercher dans la bibliothèque">';
	$html .= '<form class="bqb-search__form" role="search" method="get" action="' . esc_url( $action ) . '">';

	$html .= '<div class="bqb-search__top">';
	$html .= '<div class="bqb-mode" role="group" aria-label="Type de recherche">'
		. '<button type="button" class="bqb-mode__btn" data-bqb-mode="simple" aria-pressed="' . ( $advanced ? 'false' : 'true' ) . '">Recherche simple</button>'
		. '<button type="button" class="bqb-mode__btn" data-bqb-mode="avancee" aria-pressed="' . ( $advanced ? 'true' : 'false' ) . '" aria-controls="bqb-search-avancee">' . bqb_icon( 'sliders' ) . 'Recherche avancée<span class="bqb-mode__n" data-bqb-count' . ( $active ? '' : ' hidden' ) . '>' . (int) $active . '</span></button>'
		. '</div>';
	$html .= '<p class="bqb-search__stats"><span><strong>' . number_format_i18n( $docs ) . '</strong> ' . _n( 'document', 'documents', $docs ) . '</span>'
		. ( ! is_wp_error( $persons ) && $persons ? '<span><strong>' . number_format_i18n( (int) $persons ) . '</strong> ' . _n( 'auteur', 'auteurs', (int) $persons ) . '</span>' : '' )
		. '</p>';
	$html .= '</div>';

	$html .= '<div class="bqb-search__main">' . bqb_icon( 'search' )
		. '<label class="screen-reader-text" for="bqb-s-q">Mots-clés</label>'
		. '<input type="search" name="f_q" id="bqb-s-q" class="bqb-input" value="' . esc_attr( $val( 'q' ) ) . '" placeholder="Rechercher un titre, un auteur, un mot-clé…" />'
		. '<button type="submit" class="bqb-btn">Rechercher</button></div>';

	$html .= '<div class="bqb-search__adv" id="bqb-search-avancee"' . ( $advanced ? '' : ' hidden' ) . '>';
	$html .= '<div class="bqb-search__grid">' . $controls . '</div>';
	$html .= '<div class="bqb-search__foot"><a class="bqb-search__reset" href="' . esc_url( $action ) . '">Effacer les critères</a>'
		. '<button type="submit" class="bqb-btn">Afficher les résultats' . bqb_icon( 'arrow' ) . '</button></div>';
	$html .= '</div>';

	// L'ordre choisi dans la liste est conservé d'une recherche à l'autre.
	$html .= '<input type="hidden" name="f_tri" value="' . esc_attr( $val( 'tri' ) ) . '" />';
	$html .= '</form>';

	$html .= '<nav class="bqb-search__links" aria-label="Accès rapides"><span class="bqb-search__links-label">Accès rapides</span>';
	foreach ( $links as $link ) {
		$html .= '<a href="' . esc_url( $link[0] ) . '">' . bqb_icon( $link[2] ) . esc_html( $link[1] ) . '</a>';
	}
	$html .= '</nav></section>';

	return $html;
}

/**
 * Au-dessus des résultats : le nombre de documents et l'ordre de tri.
 */
function bqb_render_sortbar( $tri, $anchor ) {
	$keep = '';

	foreach ( bqb_filter_params() as $param ) {
		if ( in_array( $param, array( 'f_tri', 'pg' ), true ) || ! isset( $_GET[ $param ] ) || ! is_string( $_GET[ $param ] ) || '' === $_GET[ $param ] ) { // phpcs:ignore WordPress.Security.NonceVerification
			continue;
		}
		$keep .= '<input type="hidden" name="' . esc_attr( $param ) . '" value="' . esc_attr( sanitize_text_field( wp_unslash( $_GET[ $param ] ) ) ) . '" />'; // phpcs:ignore WordPress.Security.NonceVerification
	}

	$options = array( '' => 'Plus récents', 'ancien' => 'Plus anciens', 'titre' => 'Titre, de A à Z' );
	$select  = '';
	foreach ( $options as $value => $label ) {
		$select .= '<option value="' . esc_attr( $value ) . '"' . selected( 'recent' === $tri ? '' : $tri, $value, false ) . '>' . esc_html( $label ) . '</option>';
	}

	return '<form class="bqb-sortbar" method="get" action="' . esc_url( remove_query_arg( bqb_filter_params() ) . $anchor ) . '">' . $keep
		. '<label for="bqb-tri">Trier par</label><select class="bqb-input" name="f_tri" id="bqb-tri">' . $select . '</select>'
		. '<noscript><button type="submit" class="bqb-btn bqb-btn--sm">OK</button></noscript></form>';
}

/**
 * Libellé lisible d'une période : « 2004 », « De 1990 à 1999 »,
 * « Depuis 2010 », « Jusqu'en 1985 ».
 */
function bqb_year_label( $value ) {
	$range = array_map( 'intval', explode( '-', (string) $value ) );

	if ( 2 === count( $range ) && $range[0] && $range[1] && $range[0] !== $range[1] ) {
		if ( $range[0] <= 1000 ) {
			return 'Jusqu\'en ' . $range[1];
		}
		if ( $range[1] >= 2100 ) {
			return 'Depuis ' . $range[0];
		}
		return 'De ' . $range[0] . ' à ' . $range[1];
	}

	return (string) $range[0];
}

/**
 * Le catalogue de la page Bibliothèque : un en-tête qui présente le filtre
 * choisi (collection, thème, personne, organisation), puis les documents ou
 * l'annuaire des personnes et organisations.
 */
function bqb_render_catalog( $atts ) {
	$url_f    = bqb_url_filters();
	$annuaire = isset( $_GET['f_annuaire'] ) ? sanitize_key( wp_unslash( $_GET['f_annuaire'] ) ) : ''; // phpcs:ignore WordPress.Security.NonceVerification
	$role     = isset( $_GET['f_role'] ) ? sanitize_key( wp_unslash( $_GET['f_role'] ) ) : ''; // phpcs:ignore WordPress.Security.NonceVerification

	if ( in_array( $annuaire, array( 'personnes', 'organisations' ), true ) ) {
		$orgs    = ( 'organisations' === $annuaire );
		$choices = $orgs ? bqb_org_types() : bqb_person_roles();
		$role    = array_key_exists( $role, $choices ) ? $role : '';
		$title   = $orgs ? 'Organisations / Sources' : ( 'laureat' === $role ? 'Les lauréats' : 'Auteurs / Personnes' );

		$html  = '<header class="bqb-catalog__head"><span class="bqb-catalog__eyebrow">Annuaire</span><h2 class="bqb-catalog__title">' . esc_html( $title ) . '</h2>';
		$html .= '<a class="bqb-catalog__back" href="' . esc_url( bqb_library_link() ) . '">Voir tous les documents</a></header>';

		$groupe = isset( $_GET['f_groupe'] ) ? sanitize_key( wp_unslash( $_GET['f_groupe'] ) ) : ''; // phpcs:ignore WordPress.Security.NonceVerification
		$groupe = in_array( $groupe, array( 'alpha', 'annee' ), true ) ? $groupe : '';

		return $html . bqb_render_people(
			$orgs,
			$role,
			function ( $key ) use ( $annuaire ) {
				return bqb_library_link( array( 'f_annuaire' => $annuaire, 'f_role' => $key ) );
			},
			$groupe
		);
	}

	$html = bqb_render_context( $url_f );

	if ( ! $html ) {
		$html = '<header class="bqb-catalog__head"><span class="bqb-catalog__eyebrow">Catalogue</span><h2 class="bqb-catalog__title">' . esc_html( $atts['titre_catalogue'] ) . '</h2></header>';
	}

	$docs            = array_fill_keys( array_keys( bqb_short_map() ), '' );
	$docs['annee']   = '';
	$docs['acces']   = '';
	$docs['groupe']  = '';
	$docs['tri']     = '';
	$docs['nombre']  = '';
	$docs['filtres'] = 'non';
	$docs['outils']  = 'oui';
	$docs['titre']   = '';
	$docs['vide']    = 'Aucun document ne correspond à ces critères pour le moment.';
	$docs['pagination'] = 'oui';
	$docs['ancre']   = 'bqb-catalogue';
	$docs['affichage']   = 'cartes';
	$docs['sousfiltres'] = 'non';

	return $html . bqb_render_documents( $docs );
}

/**
 * En-tête du catalogue quand un seul sujet est choisi : il remplace les
 * anciennes pages séparées, avec la description, la photo ou le logo.
 */
function bqb_render_context( $url_f ) {
	$labels = array(
		'personne'     => array( 'bqb_personne', 'Auteur' ),
		'organisation' => array( 'bqb_organisation', 'Organisation' ),
		'collection'   => array( 'bqb_collection', 'Collection' ),
		'theme'        => array( 'bqb_theme', 'Thème' ),
		'secteur'      => array( 'bqb_theme', 'Secteur' ),
		'pays'         => array( 'bqb_theme', 'Pays / territoire' ),
		'nature'       => array( 'bqb_nature', 'Nature du document' ),
	);

	$term = null;
	foreach ( $labels as $short => $info ) {
		if ( ! empty( $url_f[ $short ] ) ) {
			$found = get_term_by( 'slug', $url_f[ $short ], $info[0] );
			if ( $found ) {
				$term  = $found;
				$label = $info[1];
				break;
			}
		}
	}

	if ( ! $term ) {
		return '';
	}

	$visual = '';
	$extra  = '';
	$text   = $term->description;

	if ( 'bqb_personne' === $term->taxonomy ) {
		$photo = (int) get_term_meta( $term->term_id, 'bqb_photo', true );
		$roles = array_intersect_key( bqb_person_roles(), array_flip( (array) get_term_meta( $term->term_id, 'bqb_roles', true ) ) );
		$year  = get_term_meta( $term->term_id, 'bqb_annee_prix', true );
		$prix  = get_term_meta( $term->term_id, 'bqb_prix_nom', true );
		$site  = get_term_meta( $term->term_id, 'bqb_site', true );
		$bio   = get_term_meta( $term->term_id, 'bqb_bio', true );

		$visual = $photo ? wp_get_attachment_image( $photo, 'medium', false, array( 'class' => 'bqb-context__photo', 'alt' => 'Portrait de ' . $term->name ) ) : '';
		$label  = $roles ? implode( ', ', $roles ) : $label;
		$text   = $bio ? $bio : $text;

		if ( $prix || $year ) {
			$extra .= '<p class="bqb-context__prize">' . esc_html( $prix ? $prix : 'Lauréat' ) . ( $year ? ' ' . (int) $year : '' ) . '</p>';
		}
		if ( $site ) {
			$extra .= '<p><a class="bqb-btn bqb-btn--ghost" href="' . esc_url( $site ) . '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Page personnelle</a></p>';
		}
	}

	if ( 'bqb_organisation' === $term->taxonomy ) {
		$logo  = (int) get_term_meta( $term->term_id, 'bqb_logo', true );
		$type  = get_term_meta( $term->term_id, 'bqb_type_orga', true );
		$types = bqb_org_types();
		$site  = get_term_meta( $term->term_id, 'bqb_site', true );

		$visual = $logo ? wp_get_attachment_image( $logo, 'medium', false, array( 'class' => 'bqb-context__logo', 'alt' => 'Logo ' . $term->name ) ) : '';
		$label  = ( $type && isset( $types[ $type ] ) ) ? $types[ $type ] : $label;

		if ( $site ) {
			$extra .= '<p><a class="bqb-btn bqb-btn--ghost" href="' . esc_url( $site ) . '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Site internet</a></p>';
		}
	}

	$color = '';
	if ( 'bqb_collection' === $term->taxonomy ) {
		$anc   = get_ancestors( $term->term_id, $term->taxonomy, 'taxonomy' );
		$root  = $anc ? get_term( end( $anc ), $term->taxonomy ) : $term;
		$color = $root && ! is_wp_error( $root ) ? get_term_meta( $root->term_id, 'bqb_couleur', true ) : '';
	}

	// Le chemin, pour remonter d'un niveau sans revenir en haut de page.
	$path = '';
	foreach ( array_reverse( get_ancestors( $term->term_id, $term->taxonomy, 'taxonomy' ) ) as $ancestor_id ) {
		$ancestor = get_term( $ancestor_id, $term->taxonomy );
		if ( $ancestor && ! is_wp_error( $ancestor ) ) {
			$path .= '<a href="' . esc_url( bqb_term_url( $ancestor ) ) . '">' . esc_html( $ancestor->name ) . '</a><span aria-hidden="true">›</span>';
		}
	}

	$html  = '<header class="bqb-context' . ( $visual ? ' has-visual' : '' ) . '"' . ( $color ? ' style="--bqb-accent:' . esc_attr( $color ) . ';"' : '' ) . '>';
	$html .= $visual ? '<div class="bqb-context__visual">' . $visual . '</div>' : '';
	$html .= '<div class="bqb-context__body">';
	$html .= '<span class="bqb-catalog__eyebrow">' . ( $path ? '<span class="bqb-context__path">' . $path . '</span>' : '' ) . esc_html( $label ) . '</span>';
	$html .= '<h2 class="bqb-catalog__title">' . esc_html( $term->name ) . '</h2>';
	$html .= $text ? '<p class="bqb-context__text">' . nl2br( esc_html( $text ) ) . '</p>' : '';
	$html .= $extra;

	// Sous-catégories, en raccourcis.
	$map   = bqb_term_children_map( $term->taxonomy );
	$chips = '';
	$short = bqb_short_name( $term->taxonomy );

	foreach ( isset( $map[ $term->term_id ] ) ? $map[ $term->term_id ] : array() as $child ) {
		$n = bqb_count( array( $short => array( $child->slug ) ) );
		if ( $n || bqb_settings()['afficher_vides'] ) {
			$chips .= '<a class="bqb-chip bqb-chip--nav" href="' . esc_url( bqb_term_url( $child ) ) . '">' . esc_html( $child->name ) . ' <span>' . (int) $n . '</span></a>';
		}
	}

	$html .= $chips ? '<nav class="bqb-subnav" aria-label="Sous-catégories">' . $chips . '</nav>' : '';

	return $html . '</div></header>';
}



/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * 25. Fil d'Ariane
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */

function bqb_breadcrumb( $trail ) {
	$library = bqb_library_url();
	$home    = $library ? array( $library, get_the_title( bqb_library_id() ) ) : null;

	if ( $home ) {
		array_unshift( $trail, $home );
	}

	if ( count( $trail ) < 2 ) {
		return '';
	}

	$items = array();
	$last  = count( $trail ) - 1;

	foreach ( $trail as $i => $crumb ) {
		$items[] = ( $i === $last || ! $crumb[0] )
			? '<span aria-current="page">' . esc_html( $crumb[1] ) . '</span>'
			: '<a href="' . esc_url( $crumb[0] ) . '">' . esc_html( $crumb[1] ) . '</a>';
	}

	return '<nav class="bqb-crumbs" aria-label="Fil d\'Ariane">' . implode( '<span class="bqb-crumbs__sep" aria-hidden="true">›</span>', $items ) . '</nav>';
}

function bqb_term_trail( $term ) {
	$trail = array();

	foreach ( array_reverse( get_ancestors( $term->term_id, $term->taxonomy, 'taxonomy' ) ) as $ancestor_id ) {
		$ancestor = get_term( $ancestor_id, $term->taxonomy );
		if ( $ancestor && ! is_wp_error( $ancestor ) ) {
			$trail[] = array( bqb_term_url( $ancestor ), $ancestor->name );
		}
	}

	$trail[] = array( bqb_term_url( $term ), $term->name );

	return $trail;
}

/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * 26. Fiche d'un document
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */

function bqb_related_ids( $post_id ) {
	$out = get_post_meta( $post_id, '_bqb_associes', true );
	$out = is_array( $out ) ? array_map( 'intval', $out ) : array();

	// Les documents qui citent celui-ci comme associé, en retour.
	$back = get_posts(
		array(
			'post_type'   => BQB_CPT,
			'post_status' => 'publish',
			'numberposts' => 20,
			'fields'      => 'ids',
			'meta_query'  => array( array( 'key' => '_bqb_associes', 'value' => 'i:' . (int) $post_id . ';', 'compare' => 'LIKE' ) ),
		)
	);

	foreach ( $back as $id ) {
		$list = get_post_meta( $id, '_bqb_associes', true );
		if ( is_array( $list ) && in_array( (int) $post_id, array_map( 'intval', $list ), true ) ) {
			$out[] = (int) $id;
		}
	}

	return array_values(
		array_unique(
			array_filter(
				$out,
				function ( $id ) use ( $post_id ) {
					return $id !== (int) $post_id && 'publish' === get_post_status( $id );
				}
			)
		)
	);
}

/**
 * Couverture de remplacement, aux couleurs du bloc, pour les documents
 * sans visuel : la fiche garde la même composition dans tous les cas.
 */
function bqb_cover_placeholder( $post_id, $nature ) {
	return '<div class="bqb-cover-ph" aria-hidden="true">'
		. '<span class="bqb-cover-ph__kind">' . esc_html( $nature ? $nature : 'Document' ) . '</span>'
		. '<span class="bqb-cover-ph__title">' . esc_html( get_the_title( $post_id ) ) . '</span>'
		. '<span class="bqb-cover-ph__brand">Bourse Jean-Michel Quatrepoint</span>'
		. '</div>';
}

/**
 * Sur le thème Hello, la fiche porte elle-même le titre (H1) : celui du
 * thème est masqué pour éviter un doublon.
 */
add_filter( 'hello_elementor_page_title', 'bqb_hide_theme_title', 20 );
function bqb_hide_theme_title( $show ) {
	if ( is_singular( BQB_CPT ) && bqb_settings()['fiche_auto'] ) {
		return false;
	}

	return $show;
}

function bqb_render_fiche( $post_id, $notice_html ) {
	$excerpt = get_post_field( 'post_excerpt', $post_id );
	$date    = bqb_doc_date( $post_id );
	$link    = get_post_meta( $post_id, '_bqb_lien', true );
	$file    = (int) get_post_meta( $post_id, '_bqb_fichier', true );
	$ref     = get_post_meta( $post_id, '_bqb_reference', true );
	$droits  = get_post_meta( $post_id, '_bqb_droits', true );
	$edition = get_post_meta( $post_id, '_bqb_edition', true );
	$public  = bqb_is_public( $post_id );
	$colls   = get_the_terms( $post_id, 'bqb_collection' );
	$coll    = ( $colls && ! is_wp_error( $colls ) ) ? $colls[0] : null;
	$authors = get_the_terms( $post_id, 'bqb_personne' );
	$authors = ( $authors && ! is_wp_error( $authors ) ) ? $authors : array();
	$natures = bqb_term_names( $post_id, 'bqb_nature', false, 1 );
	$nature  = $natures ? $natures[0] : '';
	$color   = $coll ? bqb_root_color( $coll->term_id ) : bqb_palette()['bordeaux'];
	$h1      = ( 'hello-elementor' === get_template() ) ? 'h1' : 'p';

	list( $url, $label, $download ) = bqb_doc_action( $post_id, 'fiche' );

	wp_enqueue_style( 'bqb-front' );
	wp_enqueue_script( 'bqb-front' );

	$html = '<article class="bqb-fiche" style="--bqb-accent:' . esc_attr( $color ) . ';--bqb-accent-soft:' . esc_attr( bqb_hex_to_rgba( $color, 0.07 ) ) . ';">';

	/* En-tête : repères, titre, auteurs, résumé, action principale. */
	$html .= '<header class="bqb-fhero"><div class="bqb-fhero__inner">';

	if ( $coll ) {
		$trail   = bqb_term_trail( $coll );
		$trail[] = array( '', get_the_title( $post_id ) );
		$html   .= bqb_breadcrumb( $trail );
	}

	$html .= '<div class="bqb-fhero__grid"><div class="bqb-fhero__main">';

	$place = '';
	if ( $coll ) {
		$number = bqb_term_number( $coll );
		if ( ! $number && $coll->parent ) {
			$parent = get_term( $coll->parent, 'bqb_collection' );
			$number = ( $parent && ! is_wp_error( $parent ) ) ? bqb_term_number( $parent ) . ' ›' : '';
		}
		$place = '<a href="' . esc_url( bqb_term_url( $coll ) ) . '">' . esc_html( trim( $number . ' ' . $coll->name ) ) . '</a>';
	}

	$html .= '<p class="bqb-fhero__eyebrow"><span class="bqb-fhero__kind">' . esc_html( $nature ? $nature : 'Document' ) . '</span>' . ( $place ? '<span class="bqb-fhero__place">' . $place . '</span>' : '' ) . '</p>';
	$html .= '<' . $h1 . ' class="bqb-fhero__title">' . esc_html( get_the_title( $post_id ) ) . '</' . $h1 . '>';

	// Auteurs avec leurs initiales, date et accès, sur une ligne.
	$html .= '<div class="bqb-fhero__by">';
	if ( $authors ) {
		$html .= '<span class="bqb-avatars" aria-hidden="true">';
		foreach ( array_slice( $authors, 0, 3 ) as $person ) {
			$photo = (int) get_term_meta( $person->term_id, 'bqb_photo', true );
			$html .= '<span class="bqb-avatar">' . ( $photo ? wp_get_attachment_image( $photo, 'thumbnail', false, array( 'alt' => '' ) ) : esc_html( bqb_initials( $person->name ) ) ) . '</span>';
		}
		$html .= '</span><span class="bqb-fhero__authors">';
		$names = array();
		foreach ( $authors as $person ) {
			$names[] = '<a href="' . esc_url( bqb_term_url( $person ) ) . '">' . esc_html( $person->name ) . '</a>';
		}
		$html .= implode( ', ', $names ) . '</span>';
	}
	if ( $date ) {
		$html .= '<span class="bqb-fhero__date">' . bqb_icon( 'calendar' ) . esc_html( $date ) . '</span>';
	}
	$html .= bqb_access_badge( $post_id ) . '</div>';

	if ( $excerpt ) {
		$html .= '<p class="bqb-fhero__lead">' . esc_html( $excerpt ) . '</p>';
	}

	$html .= '<div class="bqb-fhero__actions">';

	if ( $url ) {
		$icon  = $download ? 'download' : ( bqb_is_video( (string) $link ) ? 'play' : ( $public ? 'link' : 'lock' ) );
		$extra = ( $download && $file ) ? ' <span class="bqb-btn__meta">' . esc_html( str_replace( ' · ', ', ', bqb_file_label( $file ) ) ) . '</span>' : '';
		$html .= '<a class="bqb-btn bqb-btn--lg" href="' . esc_url( $url ) . '"' . ( $download ? ' download' : ' target="_blank" rel="noopener"' ) . '>' . bqb_icon( $icon ) . esc_html( $label ) . $extra . '</a>';
	}

	$html .= '<button type="button" class="bqb-btn bqb-btn--ghost bqb-btn--lg" data-share="' . esc_url( get_permalink( $post_id ) ) . '" data-share-title="' . esc_attr( get_the_title( $post_id ) ) . '">' . bqb_icon( 'share' ) . 'Partager</button>';
	$html .= '</div>';

	if ( ! $public ) {
		$html .= '<p class="bqb-fhero__locked">' . bqb_icon( 'lock' ) . 'Ce document est réservé. Demandez l\'accès pour le consulter.</p>';
	}

	$html .= '</div>';

	$html .= '<div class="bqb-fhero__visual">' . ( has_post_thumbnail( $post_id ) ? get_the_post_thumbnail( $post_id, 'medium_large', array( 'alt' => 'Couverture : ' . get_the_title( $post_id ), 'class' => 'bqb-fhero__cover' ) ) : bqb_cover_placeholder( $post_id, $nature ) ) . '</div>';

	$html .= '</div></div></header>';

	/* Vidéo, pleine largeur du contenu. */
	if ( $public && $link && bqb_is_video( $link ) ) {
		$embed = wp_oembed_get( $link );
		if ( $embed ) {
			$html .= '<div class="bqb-fiche__video">' . $embed . '</div>';
		}
	}

	/* Corps : texte à gauche, informations et citation à droite. */
	$rows = array(
		'Nature'               => $nature ? esc_html( $nature ) : '',
		'Collection / origine' => implode( '<br />', bqb_collection_paths( $post_id ) ),
		'Date'                 => $date ? esc_html( $date ) : '',
		'Auteurs'              => implode( ', ', bqb_term_names( $post_id, 'bqb_personne', true ) ),
		'Organisations'        => implode( ', ', bqb_term_names( $post_id, 'bqb_organisation', true ) ),
		'Secteurs'             => implode( ', ', bqb_theme_names( $post_id, 'secteur' ) ),
		'Pays / territoires'   => implode( ', ', bqb_theme_names( $post_id, 'pays' ) ),
		'Édition du prix'      => $edition ? (string) (int) $edition : '',
		'Droits'               => $droits ? esc_html( $droits ) : '',
	);

	$dl = '';
	foreach ( $rows as $label_row => $value ) {
		if ( '' !== trim( wp_strip_all_tags( $value ), ', ' ) ) {
			$dl .= '<div class="bqb-fiche__row"><dt>' . esc_html( $label_row ) . '</dt><dd>' . $value . '</dd></div>';
		}
	}

	$names = wp_strip_all_tags( implode( ', ', wp_list_pluck( $authors, 'name' ) ) );
	$cite  = trim( ( $names ? $names . ', ' : '' ) . '« ' . get_the_title( $post_id ) . ' »' . ( $ref && false === strpos( $ref, get_the_title( $post_id ) ) ? ', ' . $ref : ( $date ? ', ' . $date : '' ) ) . '. ' . get_permalink( $post_id ) );

	$themes = bqb_theme_names( $post_id, '' );
	$prose  = trim( wp_strip_all_tags( $notice_html ) );

	$html .= '<div class="bqb-fbody' . ( $prose ? '' : ' is-single' ) . '">';

	if ( $prose || $themes ) {
		$html .= '<div class="bqb-fbody__main">';
		if ( $prose ) {
			$html .= '<h2 class="bqb-fiche__h2">À propos de ce document</h2><div class="bqb-fiche__notice">' . $notice_html . '</div>';
		}
		if ( $themes ) {
			$html .= '<div class="bqb-fbody__themes"><span>Thèmes</span>' . implode( '', array_map( function ( $a ) { return str_replace( '<a ', '<a class="bqb-pillink" ', $a ); }, $themes ) ) . '</div>';
		}
		$html .= '</div>';
	}

	$html .= '<aside class="bqb-fbody__aside">';
	$html .= '<div class="bqb-fiche__infos"><h2 class="bqb-fiche__h2">Informations</h2><dl>' . $dl . '</dl></div>';
	$html .= '<div class="bqb-fiche__cite"><h2 class="bqb-fiche__h2">' . bqb_icon( 'quote' ) . 'Citer ce document</h2><p>' . esc_html( $cite ) . '</p>'
		. '<button type="button" class="bqb-btn bqb-btn--ghost bqb-btn--sm" data-cite="' . esc_attr( $cite ) . '">Copier la référence</button></div>';
	$html .= '</aside></div>';

	/* Pour continuer : documents associés, sinon la même rubrique. */
	$related = bqb_related_ids( $post_id );
	$title   = 'Documents associés';

	if ( ! $related && $coll ) {
		$query   = new WP_Query(
			array_merge(
				bqb_query_args( array( 'collection' => array( $coll->slug ), 'tri' => 'recent' ) ),
				array( 'posts_per_page' => 3, 'post__not_in' => array( $post_id ), 'fields' => 'ids', 'no_found_rows' => true )
			)
		);
		$related = array_map( 'intval', $query->posts );
		$title   = 'Dans la même rubrique';
	}

	if ( $related ) {
		$html .= '<section class="bqb-fiche__related bqb-docs is-cards"><div class="bqb-fiche__related-head"><h2 class="bqb-fiche__h2">' . esc_html( $title ) . '</h2>'
			. ( $coll ? '<a class="bqb-link-more" href="' . esc_url( bqb_term_url( $coll ) ) . '">Toute la rubrique ' . bqb_icon( 'arrow' ) . '</a>' : '' ) . '</div><div class="bqb-grid">';
		foreach ( array_slice( $related, 0, 3 ) as $id ) {
			$html .= bqb_render_doc_card( $id );
		}
		$html .= '</div></section>';
	}

	$html .= '</article>';

	return $html;
}

add_filter( 'the_content', 'bqb_filter_content', 20 );
function bqb_filter_content( $content ) {
	if ( ! is_singular( BQB_CPT ) || ! in_the_loop() || ! is_main_query() ) {
		return $content;
	}

	if ( ! bqb_settings()['fiche_auto'] ) {
		return $content;
	}

	return bqb_render_fiche( get_the_ID(), $content );
}

function bqb_sc_fiche( $atts ) {
	$atts = shortcode_atts( array( 'id' => 0 ), $atts, 'bqp_document_fiche' );
	$id   = $atts['id'] ? absint( $atts['id'] ) : get_the_ID();

	if ( ! $id || BQB_CPT !== get_post_type( $id ) ) {
		return '';
	}

	return bqb_render_fiche( $id, wpautop( do_shortcode( get_post_field( 'post_content', $id ) ) ) );
}

/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * 27. Pages automatiques des collections, thèmes, personnes, organisations
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */

add_action( 'template_redirect', 'bqb_archive_router', 5 );
function bqb_archive_router() {
	$public = array( 'bqb_collection', 'bqb_theme', 'bqb_personne', 'bqb_organisation' );

	if ( ! is_tax( $public ) ) {
		return;
	}

	$term = get_queried_object();
	if ( ! $term instanceof WP_Term ) {
		return;
	}

	$page = (int) get_term_meta( $term->term_id, 'bqb_page', true );
	$page = ( $page && 'publish' === get_post_status( $page ) ) ? $page : 0;

	// Une page dédiée existe, ou tout se trouve sur la page Bibliothèque :
	// l'adresse automatique y renvoie définitivement, filtres compris.
	if ( $page || ( ! bqb_settings()['archives_auto'] && bqb_library_url() ) ) {
		$keep   = array_intersect_key( wp_unslash( $_GET ), array_flip( bqb_filter_params() ) ); // phpcs:ignore WordPress.Security.NonceVerification
		$keep   = array_map( 'sanitize_text_field', array_filter( $keep, 'is_string' ) );
		$target = $page ? add_query_arg( array_map( 'rawurlencode', $keep ), get_permalink( $page ) ) : bqb_term_url( $term, $keep );
		wp_safe_redirect( $target, 301 );
		exit;
	}

	get_header();
	echo '<main id="content" class="site-main bqb-archive">' . bqb_render_archive( $term ) . '</main>'; // phpcs:ignore WordPress.Security.EscapeOutput.OutputNotEscaped
	get_footer();
	exit;
}

/**
 * Quand tout tient sur la page Bibliothèque, les adresses des catégories
 * redirigent : elles n'ont rien à faire dans les sitemaps.
 */
function bqb_archives_hidden( $taxonomy ) {
	return in_array( $taxonomy, array( 'bqb_collection', 'bqb_theme', 'bqb_personne', 'bqb_organisation' ), true )
		&& ! bqb_settings()['archives_auto'] && bqb_library_url();
}

add_filter( 'wp_sitemaps_taxonomies', 'bqb_core_sitemap_taxonomies' );
function bqb_core_sitemap_taxonomies( $taxonomies ) {
	foreach ( array_keys( $taxonomies ) as $taxonomy ) {
		if ( bqb_archives_hidden( $taxonomy ) ) {
			unset( $taxonomies[ $taxonomy ] );
		}
	}

	return $taxonomies;
}

add_filter( 'rank_math/sitemap/exclude_taxonomy', 'bqb_rank_math_sitemap_taxonomy', 10, 2 );
function bqb_rank_math_sitemap_taxonomy( $exclude, $taxonomy ) {
	return bqb_archives_hidden( $taxonomy ) ? true : $exclude;
}

function bqb_render_archive( $term ) {
	$labels = array(
		'bqb_collection'   => 'Collection',
		'bqb_theme'        => 'Thème',
		'bqb_personne'     => 'Auteur',
		'bqb_organisation' => 'Organisation',
	);

	$map   = bqb_term_children_map( $term->taxonomy );
	$color = '';

	if ( 'bqb_collection' === $term->taxonomy ) {
		$root  = $term;
		$anc   = get_ancestors( $term->term_id, $term->taxonomy, 'taxonomy' );
		$root  = $anc ? get_term( end( $anc ), $term->taxonomy ) : $term;
		$color = get_term_meta( $root->term_id, 'bqb_couleur', true );
	}

	wp_enqueue_style( 'bqb-front' );

	$html  = '<div class="bqb-page"' . ( $color ? ' style="--bqb-accent:' . esc_attr( $color ) . ';"' : '' ) . '>';
	$html .= bqb_breadcrumb( bqb_term_trail( $term ) );
	$html .= '<header class="bqb-hero">';

	$visual = '';
	$extra  = '';

	if ( 'bqb_personne' === $term->taxonomy ) {
		$photo = (int) get_term_meta( $term->term_id, 'bqb_photo', true );
		$roles = array_intersect_key( bqb_person_roles(), array_flip( (array) get_term_meta( $term->term_id, 'bqb_roles', true ) ) );
		$year  = get_term_meta( $term->term_id, 'bqb_annee_prix', true );
		$prix  = get_term_meta( $term->term_id, 'bqb_prix_nom', true );
		$site  = get_term_meta( $term->term_id, 'bqb_site', true );
		$bio   = get_term_meta( $term->term_id, 'bqb_bio', true );

		$visual = $photo ? wp_get_attachment_image( $photo, 'medium', false, array( 'class' => 'bqb-hero__photo', 'alt' => 'Portrait de ' . $term->name ) ) : '';
		$labels['bqb_personne'] = $roles ? implode( ', ', $roles ) : 'Auteur';

		if ( $prix || $year ) {
			$extra .= '<p class="bqb-hero__prize">' . esc_html( $prix ? $prix : 'Lauréat' ) . ( $year ? ' ' . (int) $year : '' ) . '</p>';
		}
		if ( $bio ) {
			$extra .= '<p class="bqb-hero__text">' . nl2br( esc_html( $bio ) ) . '</p>';
		}
		if ( $site ) {
			$extra .= '<p><a class="bqb-btn bqb-btn--ghost" href="' . esc_url( $site ) . '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Page personnelle</a></p>';
		}
	}

	if ( 'bqb_organisation' === $term->taxonomy ) {
		$logo  = (int) get_term_meta( $term->term_id, 'bqb_logo', true );
		$type  = get_term_meta( $term->term_id, 'bqb_type_orga', true );
		$types = bqb_org_types();
		$site  = get_term_meta( $term->term_id, 'bqb_site', true );

		$visual = $logo ? wp_get_attachment_image( $logo, 'medium', false, array( 'class' => 'bqb-hero__logo', 'alt' => 'Logo ' . $term->name ) ) : '';

		if ( $type && isset( $types[ $type ] ) ) {
			$labels['bqb_organisation'] = $types[ $type ];
		}
		if ( $site ) {
			$extra .= '<p><a class="bqb-btn bqb-btn--ghost" href="' . esc_url( $site ) . '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Site internet</a></p>';
		}
	}

	if ( $visual ) {
		$html .= '<div class="bqb-hero__visual">' . $visual . '</div>';
	}

	$html .= '<div class="bqb-hero__body">';
	$html .= '<span class="bqb-hero__eyebrow">' . esc_html( $labels[ $term->taxonomy ] ) . '</span>';
	$html .= '<h1 class="bqb-hero__title">' . esc_html( $term->name ) . '</h1>';

	if ( $term->description ) {
		$html .= '<p class="bqb-hero__text">' . esc_html( $term->description ) . '</p>';
	}

	$html .= $extra . '</div></header>';

	// Sous-catégories, en raccourcis.
	if ( ! empty( $map[ $term->term_id ] ) ) {
		$chips = '';
		foreach ( $map[ $term->term_id ] as $child ) {
			if ( $child->count || bqb_settings()['afficher_vides'] ) {
				$chips .= '<a class="bqb-chip bqb-chip--nav" href="' . esc_url( bqb_term_url( $child ) ) . '">' . esc_html( $child->name ) . ' <span>' . (int) $child->count . '</span></a>';
			}
		}
		if ( $chips ) {
			$html .= '<nav class="bqb-subnav" aria-label="Sous-catégories">' . $chips . '</nav>';
		}
	}

	$atts            = array_fill_keys( array_keys( bqb_short_map() ), '' );
	$atts['annee']   = '';
	$atts['acces']   = '';
	$atts['groupe']  = '';
	$atts['tri']     = '';
	$atts['nombre']  = '';
	$atts['filtres'] = 'oui';
	$atts['titre']   = '';
	$atts['vide']    = 'Aucun document dans cette rubrique pour le moment.';
	$atts['pagination'] = 'oui';

	$html .= bqb_render_documents( $atts, array( bqb_short_name( $term->taxonomy ) => array( $term->slug ) ) );
	$html .= '</div>';

	return $html;
}

/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * 28. [bqp_personnes] : index des personnes ou des organisations
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */

function bqb_sc_personnes( $atts ) {
	$atts = shortcode_atts( array( 'famille' => 'personnes', 'role' => '', 'type' => '', 'groupe' => '' ), $atts, 'bqp_personnes' );
	$orgs = ( 'organisations' === strtolower( $atts['famille'] ) );

	$filter_key = $orgs ? 'type' : 'role';
	$choices    = $orgs ? bqb_org_types() : bqb_person_roles();
	$current    = $atts[ $filter_key ];

	if ( '' === $current && isset( $_GET[ $filter_key ] ) ) { // phpcs:ignore WordPress.Security.NonceVerification
		$current = sanitize_key( wp_unslash( $_GET[ $filter_key ] ) ); // phpcs:ignore WordPress.Security.NonceVerification
	}
	$current = array_key_exists( $current, $choices ) ? $current : '';

	return bqb_render_people(
		$orgs,
		$current,
		function ( $key ) use ( $filter_key ) {
			return $key ? add_query_arg( $filter_key, $key ) : remove_query_arg( $filter_key );
		},
		$atts['groupe']
	);
}

/**
 * L'annuaire des personnes ou des organisations. $chip_url donne l'adresse
 * de chaque pastille de filtre (clé vide pour « Tous »).
 */
function bqb_render_people( $orgs, $current, $chip_url, $groupe = '' ) {
	$tax     = $orgs ? 'bqb_organisation' : 'bqb_personne';
	$choices = $orgs ? bqb_org_types() : bqb_person_roles();
	$args    = array( 'taxonomy' => $tax, 'hide_empty' => false, 'orderby' => 'name' );

	if ( $current ) {
		$args['meta_query'] = $orgs
			? array( array( 'key' => 'bqb_type_orga', 'value' => $current ) )
			: array( array( 'key' => 'bqb_roles', 'value' => '"' . $current . '"', 'compare' => 'LIKE' ) );
	}

	$terms = get_terms( $args );
	$terms = is_wp_error( $terms ) ? array() : $terms;

	// Sans filtre, on n'affiche que les fiches reliées à au moins un document,
	// ou les lauréats, qui ont leur place même sans document en ligne.
	if ( ! $current ) {
		$terms = array_values(
			array_filter(
				$terms,
				function ( $t ) use ( $orgs ) {
					return $t->count || ( ! $orgs && in_array( 'laureat', (array) get_term_meta( $t->term_id, 'bqb_roles', true ), true ) );
				}
			)
		);
	}

	wp_enqueue_style( 'bqb-front' );

	$html  = '<section class="bqb-people">';
	$html .= '<nav class="bqb-people__filters">';
	$html .= '<a class="bqb-chip bqb-chip--nav' . ( $current ? '' : ' is-on' ) . '" href="' . esc_url( $chip_url( '' ) ) . '">Tous</a>';
	foreach ( $choices as $key => $label ) {
		$html .= '<a class="bqb-chip bqb-chip--nav' . ( $current === $key ? ' is-on' : '' ) . '" href="' . esc_url( $chip_url( $key ) ) . '">' . esc_html( $label ) . '</a>';
	}
	$html .= '</nav>';

	if ( ! $terms ) {
		return $html . '<p class="bqb-docs__empty">Aucune fiche pour le moment.</p></section>';
	}

	$groupe  = $groupe ? $groupe : ( ( ! $orgs && 'laureat' === $current ) ? 'annee' : 'alpha' );
	$buckets = array();

	// Peu de fiches : une seule grille, sans lettres qui allongent la page.
	if ( 'alpha' === $groupe && count( $terms ) <= 24 ) {
		$groupe = 'aucun';
	}

	foreach ( $terms as $t ) {
		if ( 'aucun' === $groupe ) {
			$key = '';
		} elseif ( 'annee' === $groupe ) {
			$year = get_term_meta( $t->term_id, 'bqb_annee_prix', true );
			$key  = $year ? 'Promotion ' . (int) $year : 'Année non renseignée';
		} else {
			$key = strtoupper( substr( remove_accents( $t->name ), 0, 1 ) );
		}
		$buckets[ $key ][] = $t;
	}

	'annee' === $groupe ? krsort( $buckets ) : ksort( $buckets );

	foreach ( $buckets as $key => $list ) {
		$html .= '<div class="bqb-group">' . ( '' !== $key ? '<h3 class="bqb-group__title">' . esc_html( $key ) . '</h3>' : '' ) . '<div class="bqb-people__grid">';

		foreach ( $list as $t ) {
			$img_id = (int) get_term_meta( $t->term_id, $orgs ? 'bqb_logo' : 'bqb_photo', true );
			$sub    = array();

			if ( $orgs ) {
				$type = get_term_meta( $t->term_id, 'bqb_type_orga', true );
				if ( isset( $choices[ $type ] ) ) {
					$sub[] = $choices[ $type ];
				}
			} else {
				$sub = array_values( array_intersect_key( $choices, array_flip( (array) get_term_meta( $t->term_id, 'bqb_roles', true ) ) ) );
			}

			$initials = '';
			foreach ( preg_split( '/[\s\-]+/u', $t->name ) as $part ) {
				$initials .= function_exists( 'mb_substr' ) ? mb_substr( $part, 0, 1 ) : substr( $part, 0, 1 );
				if ( strlen( $initials ) >= 2 ) {
					break;
				}
			}

			$html .= '<a class="bqb-person' . ( $orgs ? ' is-org' : '' ) . '" href="' . esc_url( bqb_term_url( $t ) ) . '">';
			$html .= '<span class="bqb-person__visual">' . ( $img_id ? wp_get_attachment_image( $img_id, 'thumbnail', false, array( 'alt' => '' ) ) : '<span class="bqb-person__initials">' . esc_html( strtoupper( $initials ) ) . '</span>' ) . '</span>';
			$html .= '<span class="bqb-person__body"><strong>' . esc_html( $t->name ) . '</strong>';
			if ( $sub ) {
				$html .= '<em>' . esc_html( implode( ', ', $sub ) ) . '</em>';
			}
			$html .= '<span class="bqb-person__count">' . sprintf( _n( '%s document', '%s documents', (int) $t->count ), (int) $t->count ) . '</span></span>';
			$html .= '</a>';
		}

		$html .= '</div></div>';
	}

	return $html . '</section>';
}

/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * [bqp_vitrine] : la bibliothèque mise en avant, par exemple sur l'accueil
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */

/**
 * Même langage que la page Bibliothèque : un bandeau bordeaux avec la
 * recherche, puis les espaces en cartes numérotées qui servent d'onglets.
 * Chaque onglet ouvre un panneau : présentation, rubriques en raccourcis,
 * trois documents et « Voir les N documents ». Tout est rendu d'avance :
 * changer d'onglet est instantané.
 */
function bqb_sc_vitrine( $atts ) {
	$atts = shortcode_atts(
		array(
			'titre'      => 'La bibliothèque numérique',
			'texte'      => 'Les sources, les travaux et les publications consacrés à la souveraineté économique et industrielle.',
			'nombre'     => '3',
			'onglets'    => 'collections',
			'bouton'     => 'Explorer la bibliothèque',
			'nouveautes' => 'oui',
			'recherche'  => 'oui',
			'rubriques'  => 'oui',
		),
		$atts,
		'bqp_vitrine'
	);

	$number   = max( 1, min( 6, (int) $atts['nombre'] ) );
	$themes   = ( 'themes' === strtolower( remove_accents( $atts['onglets'] ) ) );
	$tax      = $themes ? 'bqb_theme' : 'bqb_collection';
	$short    = $themes ? 'theme' : 'collection';
	$map      = bqb_term_children_map( $tax );
	$library  = bqb_library_url();
	$settings = bqb_settings();
	$tabs     = array();

	static $instance = 0;
	$instance++;

	if ( 'non' !== strtolower( $atts['nouveautes'] ) ) {
		$tabs[] = array(
			'num'   => '',
			'name'  => 'Nouveautés',
			'crit'  => array(),
			'url'   => $library ? bqb_library_link() : '',
			'text'  => 'Les derniers documents ajoutés à la bibliothèque.',
			'color' => bqb_palette()['bordeaux'],
			'term'  => null,
		);
	}

	foreach ( isset( $map[0] ) ? $map[0] : array() as $i => $root ) {
		$tabs[] = array(
			'num'   => sprintf( '%02d', $i + 1 ),
			'name'  => $root->name,
			'crit'  => array( $short => array( $root->slug ) ),
			'url'   => bqb_term_url( $root ),
			'text'  => $root->description,
			'color' => $themes ? $settings['bloc5_couleur'] : bqb_root_color( $root->term_id ),
			'term'  => $root,
		);
	}

	$panels = '';
	$nav    = '';
	$k      = 0;

	foreach ( $tabs as $tab ) {
		$query = new WP_Query(
			array_merge(
				bqb_query_args( array_merge( $tab['crit'], array( 'tri' => 'recent' ) ) ),
				array(
					'posts_per_page' => $number,
					'no_found_rows'  => false,
				)
			)
		);

		if ( ! $query->have_posts() ) {
			continue;
		}

		$id    = 'bqb-vitrine-' . $instance . '-' . $k;
		$on    = ( 0 === $k );
		$count = (int) $query->found_posts;
		$style = bqb_block_style( $tab['color'] );

		$nav .= '<button type="button" class="bqb-vtab' . ( $on ? ' is-on' : '' ) . '" role="tab" id="' . esc_attr( $id ) . '-tab" aria-controls="' . esc_attr( $id ) . '" aria-selected="' . ( $on ? 'true' : 'false' ) . '" tabindex="' . ( $on ? '0' : '-1' ) . '" style="' . $style . '" data-bqb-vtab>'
			. '<span class="bqb-vtab__num" aria-hidden="true">' . ( $tab['num'] ? esc_html( $tab['num'] ) : bqb_icon( 'clock' ) ) . '</span>'
			. '<span class="bqb-vtab__name">' . esc_html( $tab['name'] ) . '</span>'
			. '<span class="bqb-vtab__count">' . sprintf( _n( '%s document', '%s documents', $count ), number_format_i18n( $count ) ) . '</span>'
			. '</button>';

		// Les rubriques de l'espace, en raccourcis vers la page Bibliothèque.
		$chips = '';
		if ( $tab['term'] && 'non' !== strtolower( $atts['rubriques'] ) ) {
			$sub = 0;
			foreach ( isset( $map[ $tab['term']->term_id ] ) ? $map[ $tab['term']->term_id ] : array() as $child ) {
				$sub++;
				$n   = bqb_count( array( $short => array( $child->slug ) ) );
				$url = bqb_term_url( $child );
				if ( $n && $url ) {
					$chips .= '<a class="bqb-pillink" href="' . esc_url( $url ) . '">' . ( $themes ? '' : '<em>' . esc_html( $tab['num'] ? (int) $tab['num'] . '.' . $sub : '' ) . '</em>' ) . esc_html( $themes ? bqb_short_label( $child, $tab['term'] ) : $child->name ) . '<span>' . (int) $n . '</span></a>';
				}
			}
		}

		$panels .= '<div class="bqb-vpanel" role="tabpanel" id="' . esc_attr( $id ) . '" aria-labelledby="' . esc_attr( $id ) . '-tab" style="' . $style . '"' . ( $on ? '' : ' hidden' ) . '>';
		$panels .= '<div class="bqb-vpanel__head"><div class="bqb-vpanel__intro">'
			. '<span class="bqb-vpanel__eyebrow">' . ( $tab['num'] ? 'Espace ' . esc_html( $tab['num'] ) : 'À la une' ) . '</span>'
			. '<h3 class="bqb-vpanel__title">' . esc_html( $tab['name'] ) . '</h3>'
			. ( $tab['text'] ? '<p class="bqb-vpanel__text">' . esc_html( $tab['text'] ) . '</p>' : '' )
			. '</div>';
		if ( $tab['url'] ) {
			$panels .= '<a class="bqb-btn" href="' . esc_url( $tab['url'] ) . '">' . ( $tab['term'] ? 'Voir les ' . number_format_i18n( $count ) . ' documents' : 'Voir tous les documents' ) . bqb_icon( 'arrow' ) . '</a>';
		}
		$panels .= '</div>';
		$panels .= $chips ? '<nav class="bqb-vpanel__rubs" aria-label="Rubriques de « ' . esc_attr( $tab['name'] ) . ' »"><span class="bqb-vpanel__label">Rubriques</span>' . $chips . '</nav>' : '';
		$panels .= '<div class="bqb-grid bqb-grid--' . (int) min( 3, $number ) . '">';
		foreach ( wp_list_pluck( $query->posts, 'ID' ) as $post_id ) {
			$panels .= bqb_render_doc_card( $post_id, 'En savoir plus' );
		}
		$panels .= '</div></div>';

		$k++;
		wp_reset_postdata();
	}

	if ( ! $panels ) {
		return '';
	}

	wp_enqueue_style( 'bqb-front' );
	wp_enqueue_script( 'bqb-front' );

	$docs    = bqb_count( array() );
	$persons = wp_count_terms( array( 'taxonomy' => 'bqb_personne', 'hide_empty' => true ) );

	$html  = '<section class="bqb-vitrine">';
	$html .= '<header class="bqb-vhero' . ( $k > 1 ? ' has-tabs' : '' ) . '"><div class="bqb-vhero__top"><div class="bqb-vhero__intro">';
	$html .= '<span class="bqb-vhero__eyebrow">Bourse Jean-Michel Quatrepoint</span>';
	$html .= $atts['titre'] ? '<h2 class="bqb-vhero__title">' . esc_html( $atts['titre'] ) . '</h2>' : '';
	$html .= $atts['texte'] ? '<p class="bqb-vhero__text">' . esc_html( $atts['texte'] ) . '</p>' : '';
	$html .= '</div><p class="bqb-vhero__stats"><span><strong>' . number_format_i18n( $docs ) . '</strong> ' . _n( 'document', 'documents', $docs ) . '</span>'
		. ( ! is_wp_error( $persons ) && $persons ? '<span><strong>' . number_format_i18n( (int) $persons ) . '</strong> ' . _n( 'auteur', 'auteurs', (int) $persons ) . '</span>' : '' )
		. '</p></div>';

	if ( $library && 'non' !== strtolower( $atts['recherche'] ) ) {
		$html .= '<form class="bqb-vhero__search" role="search" method="get" action="' . esc_url( bqb_library_link() ) . '">' . bqb_icon( 'search' )
			. '<label class="screen-reader-text" for="bqb-vs-' . (int) $instance . '">Rechercher dans la bibliothèque</label>'
			. '<input type="search" name="f_q" id="bqb-vs-' . (int) $instance . '" placeholder="Rechercher un titre, un auteur, un mot-clé…" />'
			. '<button type="submit" class="bqb-btn">Rechercher</button></form>';
	}

	if ( $library ) {
		$html .= '<div class="bqb-vhero__links">'
			. ( $atts['bouton'] ? '<a class="bqb-vhero__cta" href="' . esc_url( $library ) . '">' . esc_html( $atts['bouton'] ) . bqb_icon( 'arrow' ) . '</a>' : '' )
			. '<a class="bqb-vhero__link" href="' . esc_url( $library . '#recherche-avancee' ) . '">' . bqb_icon( 'sliders' ) . 'Recherche avancée</a>'
			. '</div>';
	}

	$html .= '</header>';
	$html .= $k > 1 ? '<div class="bqb-vtabs" role="tablist" aria-label="Parcourir la bibliothèque">' . $nav . '</div>' : '';
	$html .= $panels;
	$html .= '</section>';

	return $html;
}

/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * 29. Styles et script du site
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */

add_action( 'wp_enqueue_scripts', 'bqb_front_assets' );
function bqb_front_assets() {
	wp_register_style( 'bqb-front', false, array(), BQB_VERSION );
	wp_add_inline_style( 'bqb-front', bqb_front_css() );

	wp_register_script( 'bqb-front', false, array(), BQB_VERSION, true );
	wp_add_inline_script( 'bqb-front', bqb_front_js() );

	$needed = is_singular( BQB_CPT ) || is_tax( array( 'bqb_collection', 'bqb_theme', 'bqb_personne', 'bqb_organisation' ) );

	if ( ! $needed && is_singular() ) {
		$post    = get_post();
		$content = $post ? (string) $post->post_content . (string) get_post_meta( $post->ID, '_elementor_data', true ) : '';
		$needed  = ( false !== strpos( $content, '[bqp_' ) );
	}

	// Chargé dans l'en-tête dès que la page en a besoin, pour éviter tout
	// affichage sans style le temps du chargement.
	if ( $needed ) {
		wp_enqueue_style( 'bqb-front' );
		wp_enqueue_script( 'bqb-front' );
	}
}

function bqb_front_js() {
	return <<<'JS'
(function(){
	function each(list, fn){ Array.prototype.forEach.call(list, fn); }

	function boot(){
		document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
			var btn = e.target.closest('[data-cite]');
			if (!btn) { return; }
			var text = btn.getAttribute('data-cite');
			var done = function(){ var old = btn.textContent; btn.textContent = 'Référence copiée'; setTimeout(function(){ btn.textContent = old; }, 1800); };
			if (navigator.clipboard) { navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(done, function(){ window.prompt('Référence à copier :', text); }); }
			else { window.prompt('Référence à copier :', text); }
		});

		// Adresses de recherche propres : les filtres vides ne sont pas envoyés.
		document.addEventListener('submit', function(e){
			var form = e.target;
			if (e.defaultPrevented || !form.matches('.bqb-filters, .bqb-search__form, .bqb-sortbar')) { return; }
			each(form.querySelectorAll('input, select'), function(el){ if (!el.value) { el.disabled = true; } });
		});

		// Hors page Bibliothèque aussi, un filtre choisi s'applique aussitôt.
		document.addEventListener('change', function(e){
			var el = e.target;
			if (!el.matches || !el.matches('.bqb-filters select, .bqb-sortbar select') || el.closest('[data-bqb-lib]')) { return; }
			if (el.form.requestSubmit) { el.form.requestSubmit(); } else { el.form.submit(); }
		});

		// Partager une fiche : menu natif du téléphone, sinon copie du lien.
		document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
			var btn = e.target.closest('[data-share]');
			if (!btn) { return; }
			var url = btn.getAttribute('data-share');
			if (navigator.share) { navigator.share({ title: btn.getAttribute('data-share-title') || document.title, url: url }).catch(function(){}); return; }
			var label = btn.lastChild;
			var done = function(){ var old = label.textContent; label.textContent = 'Lien copié'; setTimeout(function(){ label.textContent = old; }, 1800); };
			if (navigator.clipboard) { navigator.clipboard.writeText(url).then(done, function(){ window.prompt('Lien à copier :', url); }); } else { window.prompt('Lien à copier :', url); }
		});

		// Sur mobile, les filtres se déplient à la demande.
		document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
			var t = e.target.closest('[data-bqb-toggle]');
			if (!t) { return; }
			var open = t.form.classList.toggle('is-open');
			t.setAttribute('aria-expanded', open ? 'true' : 'false');
		});

		// Vitrine : onglets, à la souris et au clavier.
		document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
			var tab = e.target.closest('[data-bqb-vtab]');
			if (!tab) { return; }
			each(tab.parentNode.querySelectorAll('[data-bqb-vtab]'), function(t){
				var on = (t === tab);
				t.classList.toggle('is-on', on);
				t.setAttribute('aria-selected', on ? 'true' : 'false');
				t.tabIndex = on ? 0 : -1;
				var panel = document.getElementById(t.getAttribute('aria-controls'));
				if (panel) { panel.hidden = !on; }
			});
		});
		document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
			var tab = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-bqb-vtab]');
			if (!tab || ('ArrowRight' !== e.key && 'ArrowLeft' !== e.key)) { return; }
			var tabs = Array.prototype.slice.call(tab.parentNode.querySelectorAll('[data-bqb-vtab]'));
			var next = tabs[(tabs.indexOf(tab) + ('ArrowRight' === e.key ? 1 : -1) + tabs.length) % tabs.length];
			e.preventDefault();
			next.focus();
			next.click();
		});

		function fetchPage(href){
			return fetch(href, { credentials: 'same-origin' })
				.then(function(r){ if (!r.ok) { throw new Error(r.status); } return r.text(); })
				.then(function(html){ return new DOMParser().parseFromString(html, 'text/html'); });
		}

		function sameList(doc, list){
			return doc.querySelector('[data-bqb-list="' + list.getAttribute('data-bqb-list') + '"]');
		}

		// « Afficher plus » : les documents suivants s'ajoutent à la liste.
		document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
			var a = e.target.closest('[data-bqb-more]');
			if (!a || e.defaultPrevented || e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey || !window.fetch || !window.DOMParser) { return; }
			var list = a.closest('[data-bqb-list]');
			var nav  = a.closest('.bqb-more');
			if (!list || !nav) { return; }
			e.preventDefault();
			nav.classList.add('is-loading');
			fetchPage(a.href).then(function(doc){
				var fresh  = sameList(doc, list);
				var items  = fresh ? fresh.querySelector('[data-bqb-items]') : null;
				var target = list.querySelector('[data-bqb-items]');
				if (!items || !target) { throw new Error('liste'); }
				var first = items.firstElementChild;
				while (items.firstChild) { target.appendChild(items.firstChild); }
				var next = fresh.querySelector('.bqb-more');
				if (next) { nav.parentNode.replaceChild(next, nav); } else { nav.parentNode.removeChild(nav); }
				var link = first ? first.querySelector('a') : null;
				if (link) { link.focus({ preventScroll: true }); }
			}).catch(function(){ window.location.href = a.href; });
		});

		// Onglets des sous-catégories : la liste se met à jour sur place.
		document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
			var a = e.target.closest('[data-bqb-refresh]');
			if (!a || e.defaultPrevented || e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey || !window.fetch || !window.DOMParser) { return; }
			var list = a.closest('[data-bqb-list]');
			if (!list) { return; }
			e.preventDefault();
			list.classList.add('is-loading');
			fetchPage(a.href).then(function(doc){
				var fresh = sameList(doc, list);
				if (!fresh) { throw new Error('liste'); }
				list.innerHTML = fresh.innerHTML;
				list.classList.remove('is-loading');
				if (window.history && history.pushState) { history.pushState({ bqbList: 1 }, '', a.href); }
				if (list.getBoundingClientRect().top < 0) { list.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' }); }
			}).catch(function(){ window.location.href = a.href; });
		});

		if (document.querySelector('[data-bqb-refresh]') && !document.querySelector('[data-bqb-lib]') && window.history && history.replaceState) {
			history.replaceState({ bqbList: 1 }, '', location.href);
			window.addEventListener('popstate', function(e){ if (e.state && e.state.bqbList) { window.location.reload(); } });
		}

		// Barre de recherche : passage du mode simple au mode avancé.
		var search = document.querySelector('[data-bqb-search]');
		var sform  = search ? search.querySelector('form') : null;
		var adv    = search ? search.querySelector('.bqb-search__adv') : null;

		function advFields(){ return adv ? adv.querySelectorAll('select[name], input[name]') : []; }

		function countAdv(){
			if (!adv) { return 0; }
			var n = 0;
			each(adv.querySelectorAll('.bqb-field'), function(box){
				var set = Array.prototype.some.call(box.querySelectorAll('[name]'), function(x){ return !!x.value; });
				box.classList.toggle('is-set', set);
				if (set) { n++; }
			});
			var badge = search.querySelector('[data-bqb-count]');
			if (badge) { badge.textContent = n; badge.hidden = !n; }
			return n;
		}

		function setMode(advanced){
			if (!search) { return; }
			search.classList.toggle('is-advanced', advanced);
			adv.hidden = !advanced;
			each(search.querySelectorAll('[data-bqb-mode]'), function(b){
				b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', ('avancee' === b.getAttribute('data-bqb-mode')) === advanced ? 'true' : 'false');
			});
		}

		if (search && sform && adv) {
			search.addEventListener('click', function(e){
				var b = e.target.closest('[data-bqb-mode]');
				if (!b) { return; }
				var advanced = 'avancee' === b.getAttribute('data-bqb-mode');
				setMode(advanced);
				if (advanced) { var first = adv.querySelector('select'); if (first) { first.focus(); } }
				else { sform.querySelector('[name="f_q"]').focus(); }
			});
			adv.addEventListener('change', countAdv);

			// En recherche simple, les critères avancés ne sont pas envoyés.
			sform.addEventListener('submit', function(){
				if (search.classList.contains('is-advanced')) { return; }
				each(advFields(), function(el){ el.disabled = true; });
				setTimeout(function(){ each(advFields(), function(el){ el.disabled = false; }); }, 0);
			});
		}

		// « Affiner par secteur, pays… » : ouvre la recherche avancée sur ce champ.
		document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
			var a = e.target.closest('[data-bqb-focus]');
			if (!a || !sform) { return; }
			var field = sform.querySelector('[name="' + a.getAttribute('data-bqb-focus') + '"]');
			if (!field) { return; }
			e.preventDefault();
			if (adv && adv.contains(field)) { setMode(true); }
			search.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
			setTimeout(function(){ field.focus({ preventScroll: true }); }, 450);
		});

		// Les espaces : une carte ouvre son panneau de rubriques, juste sous
		// la rangée de cartes ; une rubrique déplie ses sous-catégories.
		var explore = document.querySelector('[data-bqb-explore]');

		function placePanel(card, panel){
			var top = card.offsetTop, last = card;
			each(card.parentNode.querySelectorAll('.bqb-ecard'), function(c){ if (c.offsetTop === top) { last = c; } });
			if (last.nextElementSibling !== panel) { last.parentNode.insertBefore(panel, last.nextElementSibling); }
		}

		function toggleSpace(card, focus){
			var grid  = card.parentNode;
			var panel = document.getElementById(card.getAttribute('aria-controls'));
			var open  = 'true' !== card.getAttribute('aria-expanded');
			each(grid.querySelectorAll('.bqb-ecard'), function(c){ c.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false'); c.classList.remove('is-on'); });
			each(grid.querySelectorAll('.bqb-epanel'), function(p){ p.hidden = true; });
			if (!open || !panel) { return; }
			card.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
			card.classList.add('is-on');
			placePanel(card, panel);
			panel.hidden = false;
			if (!focus) { return; }
			var title = panel.querySelector('.bqb-epanel__title');
			if (title) { title.focus({ preventScroll: true }); }
			var top = card.getBoundingClientRect().top;
			if (top < 0 || top > window.innerHeight * 0.45) { card.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' }); }
		}

		function cardOf(panel){ return explore.querySelector('[aria-controls="' + panel.id + '"]'); }

		if (explore) {
			explore.addEventListener('click', function(e){
				var card = e.target.closest('[data-bqb-open]');
				if (card) {
					if (e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey || e.shiftKey || e.button) { return; }
					e.preventDefault();
					toggleSpace(card, true);
					return;
				}
				var close = e.target.closest('[data-bqb-close]');
				if (close) {
					var c = cardOf(close.closest('.bqb-epanel'));
					if (c) { toggleSpace(c, false); c.focus(); }
					return;
				}
				var rub = e.target.closest('[data-bqb-rub]');
				if (rub) {
					var on = 'true' !== rub.getAttribute('aria-expanded');
					rub.setAttribute('aria-expanded', on ? 'true' : 'false');
					rub.parentNode.classList.toggle('is-open', on);
					document.getElementById(rub.getAttribute('aria-controls')).hidden = !on;
				}
			});

			explore.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
				if (' ' === e.key && e.target.matches('[data-bqb-open]')) { e.preventDefault(); e.target.click(); return; }
				if ('Escape' !== e.key) { return; }
				var panel = e.target.closest('.bqb-epanel');
				var c = panel ? cardOf(panel) : null;
				if (c) { toggleSpace(c, false); c.focus(); }
			});

			// La rangée change avec la largeur de l'écran : le panneau suit.
			var resizeTimer = null;
			var replace = function(){
				var card = explore.querySelector('.bqb-ecard[aria-expanded="true"]');
				var panel = card ? document.getElementById(card.getAttribute('aria-controls')) : null;
				if (!panel) { return; }
				panel.hidden = true;
				placePanel(card, panel);
				panel.hidden = false;
			};
			window.addEventListener('resize', function(){ clearTimeout(resizeTimer); resizeTimer = setTimeout(replace, 150); });
			replace();
		}

		// Après chaque mise à jour du catalogue : la recherche et les espaces
		// reflètent les filtres de l'adresse.
		function sync(){
			var params = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
			if (sform) {
				var range = (params.get('f_annee') || '').split('-');
				each(sform.querySelectorAll('[name^="f_"]'), function(el){
					var v = params.get(el.name) || '';
					if (!v && range[0] && 'f_de' === el.name) { v = range[0] > 1000 ? range[0] : ''; }
					if (!v && range[0] && 'f_jusqua' === el.name) { v = range[1] ? (range[1] < 2100 ? range[1] : '') : range[0]; }
					el.value = v;
					if (el.value !== v) { el.value = ''; }
				});
				if (countAdv()) { setMode(true); }
			}
			if (explore) {
				each(explore.querySelectorAll('[data-bqb-key]'), function(a){
					var k = a.getAttribute('data-bqb-key').split(':');
					a.classList.toggle('is-current', params.get('f_' + k[0]) === k[1]);
				});
			}
		}

		if (search) {
			countAdv();
			// Arrivée par le lien « Recherche avancée » (vitrine, menu) : mode avancé.
			if ('#recherche-avancee' === location.hash) { setMode(true); }
		}

		// Page Bibliothèque : la recherche, les espaces, l'annuaire, le tri et
		// la pagination mettent à jour le catalogue sans recharger la page.
		var lib = document.querySelector('[data-bqb-lib]');
		var cat = lib ? lib.querySelector('[data-bqb-catalog]') : null;

		if (!cat || !window.fetch || !window.DOMParser || !window.URLSearchParams || !(window.history && history.pushState)) { return; }

		var busy = null;

		function samePage(url){ return url.origin === location.origin && url.pathname === location.pathname; }

		function reveal(){
			var top = cat.getBoundingClientRect().top;
			if (top < 0 || top > window.innerHeight * 0.4) { cat.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' }); }
		}

		function load(href, push, after){
			if (busy && busy.abort) { busy.abort(); }
			busy = window.AbortController ? new AbortController() : null;
			cat.classList.add('is-loading');
			cat.setAttribute('aria-busy', 'true');

			fetch(href, { credentials: 'same-origin', signal: busy ? busy.signal : undefined })
				.then(function(r){ if (!r.ok) { throw new Error(r.status); } return r.text(); })
				.then(function(html){
					var fresh = new DOMParser().parseFromString(html, 'text/html').querySelector('[data-bqb-catalog]');
					if (!fresh) { throw new Error('catalogue'); }
					cat.innerHTML = fresh.innerHTML;
					cat.classList.remove('is-loading');
					cat.removeAttribute('aria-busy');
					if (push) { history.pushState({ bqb: 1 }, '', href); }
					sync();
					if (after) { after(); return; }
					var title = cat.querySelector('.bqb-catalog__title');
					if (title) { title.setAttribute('tabindex', '-1'); title.focus({ preventScroll: true }); }
					reveal();
				})
				.catch(function(err){
					if (err && 'AbortError' === err.name) { return; }
					window.location.href = href;
				});
		}

		history.replaceState({ bqb: 1 }, '', location.href);
		window.addEventListener('popstate', function(e){ if (e.state && e.state.bqb) { load(location.href, false); } });

		lib.addEventListener('click', function(e){
			var a = e.target.closest('a[href]');
			if (!a || e.defaultPrevented || e.button || e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey || e.shiftKey || e.altKey || '_blank' === a.target) { return; }
			if (a.hasAttribute('data-bqb-more')) { return; }

			var url = new URL(a.href, location.href);
			if (!samePage(url) || '#bqb-catalogue' !== url.hash) { return; }

			e.preventDefault();
			load(url.href, true);
		});

		lib.addEventListener('submit', function(e){
			var form = e.target;
			if (!form.matches('.bqb-search__form, .bqb-sortbar, .bqb-filters')) { return; }

			var url = new URL(form.getAttribute('action') || location.href, location.href);
			if (!samePage(url)) { return; }

			e.preventDefault();
			var params = new URLSearchParams();
			each(form.querySelectorAll('input[name], select[name]'), function(el){ if (el.value && !el.disabled) { params.append(el.name, el.value); } });
			url.search = params.toString();
			url.hash = 'bqb-catalogue';

			// Changer l'ordre ne déplace pas la page : le focus reste sur la liste.
			load(url.href, true, form.matches('.bqb-sortbar') ? function(){
				var select = cat.querySelector('.bqb-sortbar select');
				if (select) { select.focus({ preventScroll: true }); }
			} : null);
		});

		// L'ordre de tri s'applique aussitôt choisi.
		lib.addEventListener('change', function(e){
			var el = e.target;
			if (!el.matches('.bqb-sortbar select, .bqb-filters select')) { return; }
			if (el.form.requestSubmit) { el.form.requestSubmit(); }
			else { el.form.dispatchEvent(new Event('submit', { bubbles: true, cancelable: true })); }
		});
	}

	if ('loading' === document.readyState) { document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', boot); } else { boot(); }
})();
JS;
}

function bqb_front_css() {
	$p = bqb_palette();

	return '
	.bqb-lib,.bqb-docs,.bqb-fiche,.bqb-page,.bqb-people,.bqb-vitrine{
		--bqb-bordeaux:' . $p['bordeaux'] . ';--bqb-dark:' . $p['bordeaux_dark'] . ';--bqb-navy:' . $p['navy'] . ';
		--bqb-ink:#2f2f2f;--bqb-muted:#6f6f6f;--bqb-line:#E6E3E1;--bqb-soft:#FAF8F7;
		--bqb-serif:"Cormorant Garamond","Playfair Display",Georgia,serif;
		--bqb-sans:"Inter","Montserrat","Lato",-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,"Segoe UI",Roboto,Arial,sans-serif;
		font-family:var(--bqb-sans);color:var(--bqb-ink);box-sizing:border-box;
	}
	.bqb-lib *,.bqb-docs *,.bqb-fiche *,.bqb-page *,.bqb-people *,.bqb-vitrine *{box-sizing:border-box;}
	.bqb-lib a,.bqb-docs a,.bqb-fiche a,.bqb-page a,.bqb-people a,.bqb-vitrine a{color:var(--bqb-bordeaux) !important;text-decoration:none !important;}
	.bqb-lib a:hover,.bqb-docs a:hover,.bqb-fiche a:hover,.bqb-page a:hover,.bqb-vitrine a:hover{color:var(--bqb-dark) !important;}
	.screen-reader-text{position:absolute !important;width:1px;height:1px;overflow:hidden;clip:rect(0,0,0,0);}

	/* Boutons, protégés des couleurs du thème */
	.bqb-lib .bqb-btn,.bqb-docs .bqb-btn,.bqb-fiche .bqb-btn,.bqb-page .bqb-btn,.bqb-vitrine .bqb-btn{
		-webkit-appearance:none;appearance:none;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;
		padding:12px 20px !important;border:1px solid transparent !important;border-radius:3px !important;
		background:var(--bqb-bordeaux) !important;background-image:linear-gradient(180deg,var(--bqb-bordeaux),var(--bqb-dark) 170%) !important;
		color:#fff !important;font-family:var(--bqb-sans) !important;font-size:.74rem !important;font-weight:700 !important;
		letter-spacing:.1em !important;text-transform:uppercase !important;line-height:1 !important;cursor:pointer;
		box-shadow:0 8px 18px -12px rgba(49,2,12,.8);transition:filter .2s,transform .2s;
	}
	.bqb-lib .bqb-btn:hover,.bqb-docs .bqb-btn:hover,.bqb-fiche .bqb-btn:hover,.bqb-page .bqb-btn:hover,.bqb-vitrine .bqb-btn:hover{filter:brightness(1.1);transform:translateY(-1px);color:#fff !important;}
	.bqb-lib .bqb-btn--ghost,.bqb-docs .bqb-btn--ghost,.bqb-fiche .bqb-btn--ghost,.bqb-page .bqb-btn--ghost{
		background:#fff !important;background-image:none !important;border-color:var(--bqb-line) !important;color:var(--bqb-bordeaux) !important;box-shadow:none;
	}
	.bqb-lib .bqb-btn--ghost:hover,.bqb-docs .bqb-btn--ghost:hover,.bqb-fiche .bqb-btn--ghost:hover,.bqb-page .bqb-btn--ghost:hover{border-color:var(--bqb-bordeaux) !important;color:var(--bqb-bordeaux) !important;background:rgba(116,4,28,.04) !important;}
	.bqb-docs .bqb-btn--link{background:none !important;background-image:none !important;border:0 !important;box-shadow:none;color:var(--bqb-muted) !important;padding:12px 8px !important;}
	.bqb-btn__meta{font-weight:500;letter-spacing:.02em;text-transform:none;opacity:.8;}

	.bqb-input{
		width:100% !important;margin:0 !important;padding:11px 13px !important;border:1px solid #DAD6D3 !important;border-radius:3px !important;
		background:#fff !important;color:var(--bqb-ink) !important;font-family:var(--bqb-sans) !important;font-size:.92rem !important;line-height:1.4 !important;box-shadow:none !important;
	}
	.bqb-input:focus{border-color:var(--bqb-bordeaux) !important;box-shadow:0 0 0 3px rgba(116,4,28,.1) !important;outline:none !important;}

	/* Listes de documents */
	.bqb-docs__title{margin:0 0 18px;font-family:var(--bqb-serif) !important;font-size:1.9rem !important;font-weight:600 !important;color:var(--bqb-dark) !important;}
	.bqb-docs__count{margin:22px 0 10px;font-size:.74rem;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--bqb-muted);}
	.bqb-docs__empty{margin:20px 0;padding:22px;border:1px dashed #DAD6D3;border-radius:6px;background:var(--bqb-soft);text-align:center;font-style:italic;color:var(--bqb-muted);}
	.bqb-docs__list{display:flex;flex-direction:column;border-top:1px solid var(--bqb-line);}
	.bqb-doc{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:18px;padding:18px 4px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--bqb-line);}
	.bqb-doc__cover{flex:0 0 56px;}
	.bqb-doc__cover img{display:block;width:56px;height:76px;object-fit:cover;border-radius:2px;box-shadow:0 4px 10px -6px rgba(0,0,0,.4);}
	.bqb-doc__main{flex:1;min-width:0;}
	.bqb-doc__meta{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;gap:10px;}
	.bqb-doc__coll{font-size:.68rem;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--bqb-muted) !important;}
	.bqb-doc__title{margin:7px 0 5px !important;font-family:var(--bqb-serif) !important;font-size:1.3rem !important;font-weight:600 !important;line-height:1.25 !important;}
	.bqb-doc__title a{color:var(--bqb-dark) !important;}
	.bqb-docs .bqb-doc__title a:hover{color:var(--bqb-bordeaux) !important;}
	.bqb-doc__info{margin:0;font-size:.84rem;line-height:1.5;color:var(--bqb-muted);}
	.bqb-sep{margin:0 8px;opacity:.5;}
	.bqb-doc__action{flex:0 0 auto;}
	@media (max-width:640px){.bqb-doc{flex-wrap:wrap;}.bqb-doc__action{flex-basis:100%;}}

	.bqb-badge{display:inline-block;padding:3px 10px;border-radius:999px;font-size:.66rem;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.06em;text-transform:uppercase;line-height:1.5;}
	.bqb-badge--public{background:#E9F3EC;color:#1D6B3A;}
	.bqb-badge--adherents,.bqb-badge--partenaires{background:rgba(116,4,28,.08);color:var(--bqb-bordeaux);}
	.bqb-badge--sur-place{background:#F0EEEC;color:#5a5a5a;}

	.bqb-groups{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:30px;margin-top:10px;}
	.bqb-group__title{display:flex;align-items:baseline;gap:10px;margin:0 0 6px !important;font-family:var(--bqb-serif) !important;font-size:1.55rem !important;font-weight:600 !important;color:var(--bqb-dark) !important;}
	.bqb-group__title span{font-family:var(--bqb-sans);font-size:.72rem;font-weight:700;color:var(--bqb-muted);}

	.bqb-pages{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;justify-content:center;margin:30px 0 0;}
	.bqb-pages__item{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;min-width:38px;height:38px;padding:0 10px;border:1px solid var(--bqb-line);border-radius:3px;font-size:.85rem;font-weight:600;}
	.bqb-pages__item.is-on{background:var(--bqb-bordeaux);border-color:var(--bqb-bordeaux);color:#fff;}

	/* Fil d’Ariane */
	.bqb-crumbs{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;gap:8px;margin:0 0 22px;font-size:.78rem;color:var(--bqb-muted);}
	.bqb-crumbs a{font-weight:600;}
	.bqb-crumbs__sep{opacity:.5;}

	/* Pages automatiques */
	.bqb-page{max-width:1140px;margin:0 auto;padding:40px 20px 70px;}
	.bqb-hero{display:flex;gap:30px;align-items:flex-start;margin:0 0 26px;padding:0 0 26px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--bqb-line);}
	.bqb-hero__visual img{display:block;width:150px;height:150px;object-fit:cover;border-radius:50%;box-shadow:0 12px 30px -18px rgba(49,2,12,.7);}
	.bqb-hero__visual img.bqb-hero__logo{border-radius:6px;object-fit:contain;background:#fff;padding:14px;border:1px solid var(--bqb-line);box-shadow:none;}
	.bqb-hero__eyebrow{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;font-size:.7rem;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.14em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--bqb-accent,var(--bqb-bordeaux));}
	.bqb-hero__eyebrow::before{content:"";width:28px;height:2px;background:var(--bqb-accent,#C75A18);}
	.bqb-hero__title{margin:10px 0 0 !important;font-family:var(--bqb-serif) !important;font-size:clamp(2rem,4vw,3rem) !important;font-weight:600 !important;line-height:1.1 !important;color:var(--bqb-dark) !important;}
	.bqb-hero__text{max-width:70ch;margin:14px 0 0;font-size:1rem;line-height:1.7;color:#4a4a4a;}
	.bqb-hero__prize{margin:10px 0 0;font-size:.8rem;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.06em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--bqb-bordeaux);}
	@media (max-width:640px){.bqb-hero{flex-direction:column;}}
	.bqb-subnav{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;margin:0 0 24px;}

	/* Index des personnes */

/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * 30. Habillage de l'administration, aux couleurs du site
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */

function bqb_admin_css_v2() {
	return <<<'CSS'
body.post-type-bqb_document #wpbody-content{font-family:"Inter",-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,"Segoe UI",Roboto,sans-serif;}
body.post-type-bqb_document .wrap h1.wp-heading-inline,body.post-type-bqb_document .wrap > h1{font-family:"Cormorant Garamond",Georgia,serif;font-size:32px;font-weight:600;color:#31020C;}
body.post-type-bqb_document .page-title-action{border:1px solid #74041C !important;border-radius:3px !important;color:#74041C !important;background:#fff !important;font-weight:600;}
body.post-type-bqb_document .page-title-action:hover{background:#74041C !important;color:#fff !important;}
body.post-type-bqb_document .button-primary{background:#74041C !important;background-image:linear-gradient(180deg,#74041C,#4a0212) !important;border-color:#31020C !important;box-shadow:none !important;text-shadow:none !important;border-radius:3px !important;font-weight:600;}
body.post-type-bqb_document .button-primary:hover,body.post-type-bqb_document .button-primary:focus{filter:brightness(1.12);}
body.post-type-bqb_document .button:not(.button-primary){border-radius:3px;border-color:#D8D2CF;color:#74041C;}
body.post-type-bqb_document .button:not(.button-primary):hover{border-color:#74041C;color:#31020C;background:#fff;}
body.post-type-bqb_document .postbox{border:1px solid #E6E3E1;border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0 1px 2px rgba(49,2,12,.04);overflow:hidden;}
body.post-type-bqb_document .postbox .postbox-header{border-bottom-color:#EFEAE8;background:#FDFBFA;}
